Source finding context: STAT 의미의 canonical source를 하나로 정하십시오. 예를 들어 priority=stat만 권위로 두고 is_stat과 StatOrder를 제거하거나 파생/뷰로 명시하거나, StatOrder를 유지한다면 priority와 is_stat의 필수 불변식 및 매핑을 정의하십시오. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 주문 우선순위와 STAT 라우팅 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하는 목적. Source finding context: 동일 주문에서 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 타입이 서로 다른 STAT 의미를 나타낼 수 있을 때. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 서로 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 여부를 판정하면 긴급 검사 라우팅, SLA, 알림, 대시보드 집계가 불일치할 수 있습니다. Source finding context: STAT이라는 동일 주문 의미를 속성 값, boolean 파생값, 하위 타입으로 중복 선언했다. Source finding context: Order.priority enum에 stat가 포함되어 있다. Source finding context: Order.is_stat가 STAT 주문 여부를 별도 boolean으로 기록한다. Source finding context: StatOrder도 Order 하위 타입으로 응급(STAT) 검사 주문을 의미한다.
  - affected purpose: Consistently transmit order priority and STAT routing meaning in EMR/LIS integration. Source finding context: 주문 우선순위와 STAT 라우팅 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하는 목적.
  - failure condition: The same order can carry conflicting STAT implications across priority, is_stat, and type membership. Source finding context: 동일 주문에서 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 타입이 서로 다른 STAT 의미를 나타낼 수 있을 때.
  - impact: Different consumers may route urgent tests, enforce SLA, alert, or aggregate dashboards using different STAT authorities. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 서로 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 여부를 판정하면 긴급 검사 라우팅, SLA, 알림, 대시보드 집계가 불일치할 수 있습니다.
  - root hypothesis: STAT is declared as the same order meaning in separate semantic surfaces without a defined decision relationship among them.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:16-24`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:10-24`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-015`, `finding-015.cause-001`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:16`, `finding-015.cause-002`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:17`, `finding-015.cause-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:20-24`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-011`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: The declared purpose is to transmit order priority and STAT routing meaning consistently across EMR/LIS integration. If the same order can imply different STAT states through priority, is_stat, and subtype membership, downstream consumers may choose different authorities for urgent routing, SLA handling, alerts, and dashboard aggregation, producing inconsistent operational behavior.
  - root cause: The starting point is that STAT is modeled as the same order meaning in three separate semantic surfaces: an enum value, a boolean flag, and a subtype. Because no decision relationship is declared among them, none of the three is clearly authoritative and the others are not clearly derived or constrained.
  - causal path: The causal path is straightforward: Order.priority includes stat, Order.is_stat separately records STAT order status, and StatOrder also denotes an urgent STAT order. Since these all encode the same concept without a required mapping or invariant, an instance can carry conflicting STAT implications, which exposes the ontology-level inconsistency described by the issue.
  - action: Choose one canonical source for STAT meaning and then either remove the other surfaces, mark them as derived views, or define mandatory invariants that force them to agree. This decision should be made before the next stage because other routing, SLA, alerting, and reporting logic depends on a stable STAT authority.

- issue-012 (medium): Specimen produces Result should be retained as a material ontology issue: it frames a physical specimen as the producer of a result, while the model already expresses specimen lineage through Result derived_from Specimen.

Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:Specimen-to-Result relations Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:57-68,103-114 Source finding context: Specimen이 Result를 produces한다고 한 관계 이름이 검체의 의미와 맞지 않고, 이미 derived_from으로 표현된 의미와 충돌한다. Source finding context: 검체는 물리적 시료이며 결과 값을 직접 생산하는 행위자나 수행 프로세스가 아닙니다. 결과는 검사/assay 수행에서 생성되고 검체에서 유래합니다. 따라서 produces는 검체의 존재론적 유형을 과도하게 행위자로 만들며, derived_from과 같은 lineage 의미를 다른 동사로 반복해 관계 의미를 흐립니다. Source finding context: Specimen->Result produces를 제거하거나 relation kind를 source_for/provides_material_for처럼 lineage 의미로 바꾸십시오. 결과 생성 주체가 필요하다면 Assay/Test execution 같은 수행 개념을 도입해 Result produced_by 관계를 그 수행 개념에 연결하십시오. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-005 Source finding context: 검체, 검사 수행, 결과 산출의 의미 관계를 EMR/LIS 통합 권위 문서에서 정확히 구분하는 목적. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 Specimen produces Result를 결과 생성의 권위 관계로 해석할 때. Source finding context: 결과의 생성 주체와 유래 물질이 혼동되어 assay 수행, 장비/부서 책임, specimen lineage를 분리해야 하는 운영 모델에서 잘못된 데이터 흐름을 만들 수 있습니다. Source finding context: 물리적 검체의 source/lineage 의미를 production 관계 이름으로 표현했다. Source finding context: Result는 Test 수행 결과 값으로 정의되어 수행 행위의 산출물임을 암시한다. Source finding context: Result to Specimen derived_from 관계가 이미 검체 유래 의미를 표현한다. Source finding context: Specimen to Result produces 관계가 같은 검체-결과 연결을 생산 관계로 다시 표현한다.
  - affected purpose: Accurately distinguish specimen, test execution, and result production in the EMR/LIS integration authority model. Source finding context: 검체, 검사 수행, 결과 산출의 의미 관계를 EMR/LIS 통합 권위 문서에서 정확히 구분하는 목적.
  - failure condition: An implementer treats Specimen produces Result as the authoritative result-generation relationship. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 Specimen produces Result를 결과 생성의 권위 관계로 해석할 때.
  - impact: Result creation, material lineage, equipment or department responsibility, and assay execution can be conflated in downstream data flows. Source finding context: 결과의 생성 주체와 유래 물질이 혼동되어 assay 수행, 장비/부서 책임, specimen lineage를 분리해야 하는 운영 모델에서 잘못된 데이터 흐름을 만들 수 있습니다.
  - root hypothesis: The specimen-to-result lineage relationship is named as production, overexpressing a physical specimen as the producer of a result.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-005`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:57-68`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:103-114`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:57-68,103-114`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-018`, `finding-018.cause-001`, `finding-018.cause-002`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:113`, `finding-018.cause-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:114`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-012`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.structure`, `Decision whether to rename the specimen-result edge to lineage/source semantics or introduce and wire an execution concept such as Assay/Test execution as Result producer.`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-015`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: follow_up, accept_risk
  - materiality: This weakens the EMR/LIS authority model's purpose of separating specimen identity, test execution, and result production. If implementers treat Specimen produces Result as the authoritative generation relation, downstream flows can conflate material lineage with assay execution, equipment or department responsibility, and result creation.
  - root cause: The starting point is that a source/lineage relationship from specimen to result was named as production. That overexpresses the specimen as an agent or process, even though the specimen is physical source material and not the execution event that creates the result.
  - causal path: The ontology defines Result as the value produced by test performance, which points to an execution concept as the natural producer. It also already states Result derived_from Specimen, covering the specimen-lineage meaning. Adding Specimen produces Result reuses the same specimen-result connection with production semantics, creating conflict and ambiguity between source material and result producer.
  - action: Repair the relation by either renaming/remodeling the specimen-result edge as lineage/source semantics, such as source_for or provides_material_for, or by introducing and wiring an Assay/Test execution concept as the actual producer of Result. This should be coordinated with the shared missing execution/result-production concept context captured for issue-014.

- issue-013 (medium): Patient is referenced by Order.patient_ref but is not represented in the explicit workflow relations graph. This leaves Patient outside the traversable Order-to-Report relation model unless attribute references are formally documented as authoritative relationships.

  - issue statement: Patient appears as an Order attribute target but is absent from the explicit workflow relations graph. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Patient connectivity Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:15-20,31-35,108-119 Source finding context: Patient is only present as an Order attribute target and is absent from the explicit relations graph. Source finding context: The file says it is intended as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority and defines the lab pipeline from Order to Report. If the explicit relations graph is the structural contract, the patient dimension is not traversable from the workflow relations even though orders are issued for patients. Source finding context: Add explicit patient relations, for example Order -> Patient belongs_to and Patient -> Order has_many, or document that attribute refs are the authoritative relationship surface and keep relations consistent with that rule. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: Using the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration from Order through Report. Source finding context: Consumers that rely on the relations section to derive integration paths cannot connect lab orders, specimens, results, or reports to Patient. Source finding context: This weakens trust in the model as an integration authority because a core workflow participant is structurally outside the declared relation graph. Source finding context: The ontology splits structural linkage between attribute refs and the relations list without making Patient an explicit relation participant. Source finding context: Patient is declared as a standalone entity and Order.patient_ref targets Patient. Source finding context: The explicit relations list enumerates workflow edges but contains no edge whose from or to is Patient. Source finding context: A relation-graph consumer therefore has no explicit Patient path in the Order-to-Report workflow.
  - affected purpose: Using the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration from Order through Report.
  - failure condition: Consumers rely on the relations section to derive integration paths across orders, specimens, results, reports, and patients. Source finding context: Consumers that rely on the relations section to derive integration paths cannot connect lab orders, specimens, results, or reports to Patient.
  - impact: A core workflow participant is structurally outside the declared relation graph, weakening the model as an integration authority. Source finding context: This weakens trust in the model as an integration authority because a core workflow participant is structurally outside the declared relation graph.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology splits structural linkage between attribute refs and the relations list without making Patient an explicit relation participant.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:6-8`, `materialized-input.md:15-20`, `materialized-input.md:31-35`, `materialized-input.md:108-119`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:15-20,31-35,108-119`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-019`, `finding-019.cause-001`, `finding-019.cause-002`, `finding-019.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-013`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: follow_up, accept_risk
  - materiality: The ontology is intended to serve as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration from Order through Report. If consumers derive integration paths from the relations section, they cannot connect orders, specimens, results, or reports to Patient, which weakens trust in the model for a core workflow participant.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with a split between two structural surfaces: Patient is available through an Order attribute reference, while the explicit relations list is the apparent workflow graph. Because Patient is not made an explicit relation participant and no rule elevates attribute refs as graph edges, consumers have no clear authoritative Patient path.
  - causal path: The evidence path is: Patient is declared as an entity and targeted by Order.patient_ref; the explicit relations list enumerates workflow edges but includes no relation from or to Patient; therefore a relation-graph consumer cannot traverse from the lab workflow to Patient in the Order-to-Report model.
  - action: Resolve the structural authority gap by either adding explicit patient relations, such as Order belongs_to Patient and Patient has_many Order, or documenting that attribute references are the authoritative relationship surface and aligning the relations list with that rule. The decision about which surface is authoritative should come first, because it determines whether the fix is a graph change or a contract/documentation alignment change.

Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Assay connectivity Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46-60,108-119,126-127 Source finding context: Assay is not connected to the orderable Test or result-production path. Source finding context: Structurally, the model connects Test to Result and Specimen to Result, but leaves the analyzer execution concept outside that path. That makes the declared dual catalog maintenance rule hard to operationalize from the ontology. Source finding context: Add the minimal execution mapping, such as Test -> Assay maps_to/implemented_by and Assay -> Result produces, or explicitly de-scope Assay from the authoritative workflow graph until catalog consolidation is complete. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Using the ontology as an EMR/LIS concept authority where orderable catalog items and analyzer-executed items must be integrated. Source finding context: A consumer following the relations graph can connect Test to Result but cannot determine which Assay implements the Test or produces the Result. Source finding context: This creates a structural break between EMR-orderable tests and LIS/analyzer execution units, reducing actionability for integration design. Source finding context: Assay was introduced as a separate catalog concept but not wired into the explicit relations graph. Source finding context: Test is the orderable catalog unit and Assay is the analyzer execution unit. Source finding context: The notes require new items to be registered in both Test and Assay while consolidation is pending. Source finding context: The relations list connects Test, Specimen, Result, Report, Order, Department, and Staff, but has no Assay edge. Source finding context: The analyzer execution concept is therefore structurally disconnected from the result-production path.
  - affected purpose: Using the ontology as an EMR/LIS concept authority where orderable catalog items and analyzer-executed items must be integrated.
  - failure condition: A consumer follows the relations graph to connect Test to Result but needs to determine which Assay implements the Test or produces the Result. Source finding context: A consumer following the relations graph can connect Test to Result but cannot determine which Assay implements the Test or produces the Result.
  - impact: The model leaves a structural break between EMR-orderable tests and LIS/analyzer execution units. Source finding context: This creates a structural break between EMR-orderable tests and LIS/analyzer execution units, reducing actionability for integration design.

  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:46-60`, `materialized-input.md:108-119`, `materialized-input.md:126-127`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46-60,108-119,126-127`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-020`, `finding-020.cause-001`, `finding-020.cause-002`, `finding-020.cause-003`, `finding-020.cause-004`, `materialized-input.md:111-119`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-014`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.structure`, `Decision whether to wire Assay into the authoritative graph with minimal execution relations or explicitly de-scope Assay from the workflow graph.`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-015`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the declared EMR/LIS authority purpose because orderable catalog items and analyzer-executed items must be integrated. A consumer can follow existing graph edges from Test to Result, but cannot determine which Assay implements the Test or produces the Result, leaving an actionable integration gap between EMR ordering and LIS/analyzer execution.
  - root cause: The starting point is the missing governed orderable-to-executable mapping: Assay was introduced as a separate execution catalog concept, but the explicit relations graph never assigns it an implementation or result-production role. Deliberation narrowed this as a bounded structural manifestation of the broader Test-Assay boundary and mapping problem, not a separate competing root.
  - causal path: The causal chain is that Test and Assay are both present as distinct catalog concepts, notes require new items to be registered in both while consolidation is pending, yet the relations graph connects Test, Specimen, Result, Report, Order, Department, and Staff without any Assay edge. Therefore the analyzer execution concept is outside the result-production path even though it is needed to explain how an orderable Test is executed and yields a Result.
  - action: Close this before the next stage by deciding whether Assay belongs in the authoritative workflow graph. If it does, add the minimal execution relations, such as Test implemented_by Assay and Assay produces Result, aligned with the broader issue-006 Test-Assay mapping work. If Assay is not yet authoritative, explicitly de-scope it from the workflow graph until catalog consolidation is complete, so consumers are not misled by an execution concept with no operational path.

  - impact: A safety-relevant workflow concept is detached from the result/report path that would drive operational handling. Source finding context: This leaves a safety-relevant workflow concept structurally detached from the result/report path that would need to drive operational handling.
  - root hypothesis: CriticalValue combines threshold and notification state but is not represented in the explicit workflow relations.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:62-73`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `materialized-input.md:108-119`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:62-73,100-106,108-119`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-021`, `finding-021.cause-001`, `finding-021.cause-002`, `finding-021.cause-003`, `finding-021.cause-004`, `materialized-input.md:100-119`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-015`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-015.structure`, `Decision whether CriticalValue remains catalog-only with notification state moved to a separate event concept, or is explicitly split/typed with Result and actor relations.`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because the ontology is meant to act as operational concept authority for lab result workflow and LIS reporting behavior. Critical-value handling is safety-relevant; if it is detached from the Result and report path, the ontology cannot reliably drive or audit the workflow that identifies a critical result and confirms notification.
  - root cause: The starting point is that CriticalValue combines two meanings: a threshold catalog concept and result-specific notification state. Because those meanings are not separated or explicitly typed, the model has no stable relation target for produced Result instances or for notification actors.
  - causal path: Result is modeled as the produced test result with status and verifier fields, while CriticalValue is modeled as immediately notifiable dangerous ranges with notified state. The explicit relation graph connects Result to Report and Specimen, but provides no CriticalValue edge. As a result, CriticalValue handling remains only indirectly attribute-anchored through Test and is not structurally connected to actual Result workflow instances or notification actors.
  - action: Resolve the threshold-versus-event modeling decision before the next stage. Either make CriticalValue catalog-only and move notification state into a separate result-specific notification event, or explicitly split/type the concept so Result-to-critical-handling and actor notification traversal are represented. Simply adding edges to the mixed concept is not enough unless the threshold and notification-event authority distinction is also settled.

- none

Because the ontology is intended as EMR/LIS concept authority, this is a direct authority and clinical-trust risk that should be closed in the target model. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Report.result_status correction handling Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69-83,121-123; .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11 Source finding context: Clinician-trusted report status is allowed to lag corrected LIS results, which misaligns the ontology with its purpose as an EMR/LIS concept authority. Source finding context: The declared review value is suitability as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration, especially operational risk. A concept authority that explicitly lets a clinician-facing authority value remain `finalized` until a nightly batch after a result is corrected accepts a hidden tradeoff against timely clinical truth propagation. That is not merely an implementation detail: the target itself assigns trust to `Report.result_status`, so the delayed propagation weakens the document's purpose as an authority for integration behavior. Source finding context: Make the corrected/amended status a single authoritative state or require event-driven/synchronous propagation from `Result.status=corrected` to `Report.result_status=amended`. If delayed processing is unavoidable, model an explicit interim state such as `amendment_pending`, with timestamp, source result, and EMR-facing trust rules. Source finding context: A LIS result changes to `corrected` after report release but before the nightly synchronization batch updates the report status. Source finding context: The EMR-facing status that clinicians are told to trust can remain stale, so downstream integration can present corrected clinical information as finalized during the lag window. Source finding context: The model duplicates result status authority across LIS `Result.status` and clinician-facing `Report.result_status`, then permits asynchronous reconciliation for a trust-critical correction path. Source finding context: `Report.result_status` is described as the authority value shown on the report and trusted by clinicians. Source finding context: The same status meaning is also retained on `Result.status`, which is recorded by LIS. Source finding context: When `Result.status` becomes corrected, `Report.result_status` must become amended, but the synchronization batch runs only once nightly. Source finding context: The requested purpose is to judge whether this ontology is suitable as an EMR/LIS integration concept authority with operational-risk awareness. As a concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, this weakens precedence and invalid-input behavior because consumers cannot tell whether Report.result_status is an immediate authority value or an eventually consistent projection. Also specify which value is authoritative during the sync window. Source finding context: The model combines immediate status-equivalence language with an explicitly delayed synchronization mechanism without defining the consistency window. - issue-002: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: The boolean is the visible modeling failure, while the deeper shared cause is the absence of a first-class notification event/evidence concept. The issue materially weakens operational-risk traceability, but can be closed with the broader critical-value event modeling work. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / CriticalValue notification model Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100-106; .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11 Source finding context: Critical-value notification is reduced to a boolean while the actual recipient and time authority are external, weakening the ontology's operational-risk purpose. Source finding context: For an EMR/LIS integration concept authority, critical-value notification is a stakeholder- and safety-critical workflow, not a peripheral annotation. A boolean cannot represent who was notified, when, by whom, by which channel, or whether acknowledgement closed the loop. By pushing the meaningful notification evidence into an unmodeled ledger, the ontology accepts an implicit tradeoff against traceability and integration clarity without justifying it. Source finding context: Promote critical-value notification into an explicit concept such as `CriticalValueNotification` with result/test reference, recipient, notifier, notified_at, channel, acknowledgement status, and source authority. Relate it to `CriticalValue`, `Result`, `Staff`, and the EMR/LIS release workflow. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration while identifying operational risks in entities, relations, and states. Source finding context: A critical value is reported or audited and the integration needs to determine notification timing, recipient, acknowledgement, or authoritative source from the ontology. Source finding context: The ontology cannot support the clinically important closed-loop notification semantics it gestures at, so downstream systems may treat a bare boolean as sufficient evidence of completed risk handling. Source finding context: The model treats critical-value communication evidence as external operational bookkeeping instead of a first-class integration concept. Source finding context: `CriticalValue` represents immediately notifiable dangerous result ranges. Source finding context: The only notification attribute is `notified: boolean`; time and recipient are said to exist in a phone log ledger outside the modeled ontology. Source finding context: The review purpose is suitability as an EMR/LIS integration authority with operational-risk focus.

- issue-003: fix_before_release, accept_risk
  - rationale: Deliberation narrowed the issue to a decision: model TAT as governed inside ontology authority or explicitly declare the dashboard formula outside it. The defect affects reproducibility and authority, and the remaining closure need is a boundary decision rather than detailed fix design. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / deferred local metric and catalog authorities Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46-60,126-128; .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11 Source finding context: The ontology knowingly leaves catalog and TAT authority split across local teams, creating purpose drift for a document intended to be the integration concept authority. Source finding context: The requested purpose is a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration. Leaving dual catalog registration and a separate dashboard-owned TAT formula as accepted current behavior privileges local operational convenience over canonical concept authority. The problem is not that future cleanup is planned; it is that the current authority document records the split without a governing precedence rule, drift-detection obligation, or ownership boundary. Source finding context: Add explicit authority and precedence rules: define whether `Test` or `Assay` owns orderable versus executable catalog identity, how dual registration is reconciled, and which source owns TAT semantics. If the dashboard formula remains separate, model it as a derived projection with required conformance to the ontology's canonical TAT definition. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use the ontology as a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration. Source finding context: A new test/assay or TAT-consuming integration is implemented while catalog identity or TAT semantics differ between the ontology and local dashboard/catalog maintenance practices. Source finding context: Downstream consumers can implement conflicting meanings while still appearing to follow the authority document, reducing trust in the ontology as the integration source of truth. Source finding context: The ontology accepts unresolved local authority splits as current practice without encoding precedence, ownership, or reconciliation duties. Source finding context: `Test` is the orderable catalog unit, while `Assay` is the analyzer execution unit with overlapping department and specimen-kind semantics. Source finding context: The notes require new items to be registered in both places until future catalog cleanup. Source finding context: The notes define TAT from collection to release, but also say the dashboard team maintains its own calculation formula. Source finding context: The document is being reviewed for suitability as an EMR/LIS integration concept authority. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / TAT calculation authority Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:32-39, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:70-79, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121-123 Source finding context: Turnaround-time coverage is reduced to an external dashboard formula without a governed metric concept or authority. Source finding context: TAT is an operational performance value spanning specimen collection and report release. Because the artifact positions itself as the EMR/LIS authority model, leaving a cross-system metric to an external team-owned formula creates a coverage gap for a time-dependent value that consumers are likely to compare or report. Source finding context: Model TAT as a governed derived metric with source timestamps, calculation owner, inclusion/exclusion rules, effective versioning, and behavior for amended/corrected reports; alternatively declare the dashboard metric explicitly out of scope with a canonical integration boundary. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Use as a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration, including operational values derived across workflow timestamps. Source finding context: When EMR, LIS, or dashboard consumers need the canonical TAT definition or need to reproduce historical TAT after formula changes or report amendments, the ontology points to an external team-owned calculation rather than a governed concept. Source finding context: A key operational metric can diverge across consumers and cannot be reconstructed from the ontology's own authority model beyond the two raw timestamps. Source finding context: The model includes collected_at on Specimen and released_at on Report.   - rationale: The issue is a coverage/completeness gap in the authority model. It is material for downstream traceability, but less immediately blocking than status or catalog authority defects and may be addressed in the next modeling stage. Source finding context: Integrations may treat analyzed as terminal even though the artifact states post-analysis handling exists, weakening traceability and making department-specific implementations the de facto authority. Source finding context: The target delegates post-analyzed specimen handling to departmental rules instead of modeling it as part of the shared ontology. Source finding context: Specimen lifecycle terminates at analyzed. Source finding context: The ontology nevertheless acknowledges storage/disposal after analyzed as an operational process. - issue-005: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Multiple lenses converge that the mixed concept is the deeper type and authority problem behind critical-value audit gaps. Because it affects safety-relevant notification handling and relation targets, it should be corrected in the ontology target. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / CriticalValue notification coverage Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:95-101 Source finding context: Critical-value notification is represented only as a boolean and excludes notification evidence concepts. Source finding context: Critical-value communication is a controlled action in the target domain because the entity is defined as requiring immediate notification. A boolean cannot reconstruct who notified whom, when, through what channel, and with what acknowledgement, so the authority model does not cover the audit dimension needed for operational trust. Source finding context: Replace or supplement notified with a CriticalValueNotification event/entity containing critical_value/result reference, notifier, recipient, notified_at, channel, acknowledgement/status, escalation fields, and evidence/source-log linkage. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use as an EMR/LIS integration concept authority for operationally risky lab workflow states and controlled actions. Source finding context: When a critical result requires proof of notification or reconciliation between LIS, EMR, and a phone log, the model exposes only a true/false flag and no auditable event structure. Source finding context: Systems can disagree or overwrite the notification flag without preserving the evidence needed to establish clinical communication history. Source finding context: The ontology keeps critical-value notification evidence outside the modeled concept set and collapses notification into a boolean. Source finding context: CriticalValue is defined as a result range requiring immediate notification. Source finding context: The only in-model notification attribute is notified: boolean, with time and recipient delegated to a phone log. Source finding context: The model therefore lacks the actor/time/recipient/evidence concepts needed to cover the controlled notification action inside the EMR/LIS authority model. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:CriticalValue Source finding context: CriticalValue가 위험 결과 값 범위와 통보 완료 상태를 한 개념에 섞어 threshold rule과 notification event의 존재론적 유형을 혼합한다. Source finding context: 위험 결과 값 범위는 검사별 판정 기준 또는 rule이고, 통보 여부는 특정 환자 결과에 대한 사건 상태입니다. 이 둘이 하나의 entity에 있으면 '이 범위가 통보되었는가'처럼 범위 자체의 상태로 읽히며, 환자별 critical result occurrence와 기준 정의가 의미적으로 뒤섞입니다. Source finding context: CriticalValueRange 또는 CriticalRule을 기준 개념으로 두고 lower/upper bound만 보유하게 하십시오. 환자 결과에 대한 통보는 CriticalNotification 또는 CriticalResultEvent처럼 Result에 연결된 별도 사건/상태 개념으로 분리하고 notified, notified_at, recipient 등을 그곳에 두십시오. Source finding context: 검사별 위험 범위 정의에 환자별 통보 완료 여부를 저장하거나 해석할 때. Source finding context: 한 range 레코드의 notified 값이 특정 결과 사건인지 기준 자체인지 불명확해 critical value 통보 추적, 재통보, 감사 기록의 의미가 손상될 수 있습니다. Source finding context: 기준 범위(rule)와 통보 사건(event/status)을 하나의 CriticalValue 개념에 배치했다. Source finding context: CriticalValue는 즉시 통보가 필요한 위험 결과 값 범위로 정의된다. Source finding context: 같은 개념 안에 lower_bound/upper_bound와 notified boolean이 함께 존재한다. Source finding context: notified 주석은 통보 시각과 수신자가 별도 전화 기록 대장에 있다고 하여 실제 통보 사건 정보가 range 정의 바깥에 있음을 드러낸다.

Source finding context: 검체 종류가 enum과 자유 문자열로 갈라져 새 검체 세부 유형이나 외부 LIS 표준 값을 추가할 때 연속성이 깨진다. Source finding context: 검체 분류는 장비, 검사 카탈로그, 결과 해석을 잇는 확장 축이다. 현재처럼 한쪽은 폐쇄 enum이고 다른 한쪽은 문자열이면 새 LIS 검체 코드나 세분 검체가 들어올 때 enum 확장과 문자열 매핑을 동시에 수행해야 하며, 과거 `blood` 같은 넓은 값과 신규 `Serum`/`WB` 같은 세부 값의 연속적 해석이 불명확해진다. Source finding context: 검체 종류를 별도 `SpecimenType`/`SpecimenKind` 권위 엔티티 또는 코드셋으로 승격하고, 넓은 분류와 세부 코드의 계층/alias/버전을 표현한다. `Specimen`, `Test`, `Assay`는 모두 같은 코드셋을 참조하게 하고 외부 LIS 코드 매핑을 별도 속성 또는 관계로 둔다. Source finding context: 새 검체 세부 유형, 외부 LIS 검체 코드, 부서별 검체 명칭을 수용해야 하는 경우. Source finding context: 폐쇄 enum과 자유 문자열이 동시에 존재해 새 값을 넣을 때 기존 enum 수정이 필요하고, 기존 데이터와 신규 세분 코드 사이의 매핑 권위가 없어 통합 규칙의 지속성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 검체 종류 개념이 단일 권위 코드셋 없이 여러 속성 타입으로 중복 표현된다. Source finding context: `Specimen`과 `Test`는 검체 종류를 `[blood, urine, tissue, swab]` 폐쇄 enum으로 제한한다. Source finding context: `Assay`는 같은 검체 축을 자유 문자열로 기록하며 예시는 enum보다 세분화되어 있다. Source finding context: 이 구조는 새 검체 코드 추가 시 enum 구조 변경과 문자열 매핑 규칙 추가를 요구하고, 기존 넓은 값과 신규 세부 값의 연속성을 모델 안에서 보존하지 못한다.

  - rationale: The issue identifies the redundant modeling cause that can make STAT interpretation diverge under policy or interface changes. It should be resolved before dependent integration design proceeds, but it is narrower than the highest-risk result/report status defects. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / order priority and STAT extension model Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Order.priority` enum values `[routine, urgent, stat]`; `Order.is_stat` boolean note "priority와 함께 기록"; `StatOrder` is_a `Order` with `stat_reason`. Source finding context: STAT 우선순위가 enum, boolean, subtype으로 중복되어 새 우선순위 정책 추가 시 기존 구조 수정과 해석 충돌이 발생한다. Source finding context: 우선순위 정책은 병원, 부서, 인터페이스 버전에 따라 바뀌기 쉬운 변경 축이다. 현재 모델은 단순 enum 확장만으로도 boolean 및 subtype 의미와 충돌할 수 있고, `stat_reason` 같은 STAT 전용 속성 때문에 새 긴급도 범주를 추가할 때 하위 타입을 계속 늘리거나 기존 `Order` 구조를 수정해야 한다. Source finding context: 우선순위를 단일 권위 개념으로 정하고 `is_stat`는 파생값으로 명시하거나 제거한다. STAT 전용 사유는 `priority_reason` 또는 `OrderPriority` 정책 객체의 속성으로 일반화해 새 우선순위 범주가 기존 `Order` 하위 타입 추가 없이 확장되도록 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003 Source finding context: 주문 우선순위와 응급 정책 변경을 EMR/LIS 통합 모델에서 일관되게 수용하는 목적. Source finding context: 새 우선순위 범주, 기관별 STAT 사유 정책, 외부 EMR/LIS 우선순위 코드가 추가되는 경우. Source finding context: 동일 개념의 세 표현이 병렬로 확장되어야 해 권위 충돌과 기존 구조 수정이 필요해지고, 인터페이스 매핑 시 우선순위 의미가 불안정해진다. Source finding context: 같은 STAT 여부가 `Order.is_stat` boolean으로도 기록된다. Source finding context: `StatOrder` 하위 타입이 다시 STAT 주문을 별도 구조로 정의한다. Source finding context: 따라서 새 우선순위 정책은 단일 값 추가가 아니라 enum, boolean 파생 규칙, subtype 구조의 동시 변경 문제로 번진다.

- issue-009: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: The issue is a lifecycle-rule gap: completed is ambiguous as historical event versus maintained invariant after result correction. It materially affects lifecycle interpretation and should be closed before the next integration stage. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml Order completion rule Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:23, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:71, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:122, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123 Source finding context: Order.completed is triggered by all Results being final, but later corrected Results are explicitly allowed without a corresponding Order state transition rule. Source finding context: This is a temporal inter-claim conflict if completed is used as the current completion predicate for the order. A completion judgment based on mutable Result.status can be made true and later invalidated by an allowed correction event, but the lifecycle has no state that represents that invalidation. That makes the state model unsafe as an authority for integration behavior that relies on completed meaning all results are final. Source finding context: Define whether Order.completed is a historical transition event or a maintained invariant. If it is maintained, add a correction/reopened/amended state or transition on Result.status=corrected. If it is historical, rename or document completed as release-completed and add a separate current-result-finality predicate. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use as an EMR/LIS integration authority for lifecycle and state interpretation. Source finding context: An Order reaches completed after all Results are final, then a Result is later corrected as allowed by the report correction rule. Source finding context: Integrations can see Order.completed and a corrected/non-final Result at the same time, undermining invalid-input handling and lifecycle precedence for downstream consumers. Source finding context: The model defines order completion against a reversible Result.status value but does not define how order lifecycle responds when that value changes after completion. Source finding context: Order has a completed lifecycle state but no reopened, corrected, or amended order state. Source finding context: Order completion is triggered when all Results are final. Source finding context: Result.status can later become corrected, so the all-final condition can stop holding after completion.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-011: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: This is a current consistency symptom of the same redundant STAT modeling root captured in issue-008, framed from semantic authority. It remains actionable under the projection contract and should close with the canonical STAT-source decision. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:Order STAT modeling Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:10-24 Source finding context: STAT 주문 의미가 priority 값, is_stat boolean, StatOrder 하위 타입으로 동시에 모델링되어 동의어적 개념이 분리되어 있다. Source finding context: 세 요소는 모두 같은 STAT/응급 주문 의미를 가리키지만 서로의 결정 관계가 정의되지 않았습니다. priority=stat, is_stat=true, 인스턴스 타입 StatOrder가 항상 같은 의미인지 불명확해 EMR/LIS 간 우선순위 전달과 라우팅 조건의 권위 필드가 갈라집니다. Source finding context: STAT 의미의 canonical source를 하나로 정하십시오. 예를 들어 priority=stat만 권위로 두고 is_stat과 StatOrder를 제거하거나 파생/뷰로 명시하거나, StatOrder를 유지한다면 priority와 is_stat의 필수 불변식 및 매핑을 정의하십시오. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 주문 우선순위와 STAT 라우팅 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하는 목적. Source finding context: 동일 주문에서 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 타입이 서로 다른 STAT 의미를 나타낼 수 있을 때. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 서로 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 여부를 판정하면 긴급 검사 라우팅, SLA, 알림, 대시보드 집계가 불일치할 수 있습니다. ### Consensus (6/6, core-axis mode)
- issue-001 (high): Corrected LIS results can coexist with a clinician-facing Report.result_status that still reads finalized or preliminary until nightly synchronization, so the ontology leaves clinicians and EMR/LIS integrations without a determinate trusted status during the correction window.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: coverage
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, logic
  - issue statement: Corrected LIS results can coexist with clinician-facing report status that still appears finalized or preliminary until nightly synchronization runs. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Report.result_status correction handling Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69-83,121-123; .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11 Source finding context: Clinician-trusted report status is allowed to lag corrected LIS results, which misaligns the ontology with its purpose as an EMR/LIS concept authority. Source finding context: The declared review value is suitability as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration, especially operational risk. A concept authority that explicitly lets a clinician-facing authority value remain `finalized` until a nightly batch after a result is corrected accepts a hidden tradeoff against timely clinical truth propagation. That is not merely an implementation detail: the target itself assigns trust to `Report.result_status`, so the delayed propagation weakens the document's purpose as an authority for integration behavior. Source finding context: Make the corrected/amended status a single authoritative state or require event-driven/synchronous propagation from `Result.status=corrected` to `Report.result_status=amended`. If delayed processing is unavoidable, model an explicit interim state such as `amendment_pending`, with timestamp, source result, and EMR-facing trust rules. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, with attention to operational risk. Source finding context: A LIS result changes to `corrected` after report release but before the nightly synchronization batch updates the report status. Source finding context: The EMR-facing status that clinicians are told to trust can remain stale, so downstream integration can present corrected clinical information as finalized during the lag window. Source finding context: The model duplicates result status authority across LIS `Result.status` and clinician-facing `Report.result_status`, then permits asynchronous reconciliation for a trust-critical correction path. Source finding context: `Report.result_status` is described as the authority value shown on the report and trusted by clinicians. Source finding context: The same status meaning is also retained on `Result.status`, which is recorded by LIS. Source finding context: When `Result.status` becomes corrected, `Report.result_status` must become amended, but the synchronization batch runs only once nightly. Source finding context: The requested purpose is to judge whether this ontology is suitable as an EMR/LIS integration concept authority with operational-risk awareness. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml state_rules / Report.result_status consistency Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:72, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:80, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123 Source finding context: Report.result_status must both maintain the same Result.status information and be updated only by a nightly batch after correction, creating a temporal satisfiability conflict. Source finding context: This is an inter-claim conflict on mixed modality: the attribute note states a maintained equivalence, the state rule states an obligation when corrected, and the same sentence also permits delayed synchronization. As a concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, this weakens precedence and invalid-input behavior because consumers cannot tell whether Report.result_status is an immediate authority value or an eventually consistent projection. Source finding context: Make the modality explicit: either define Report.result_status as eventually consistent with a bounded stale interval and freshness timestamp, or require synchronous update/transactional amendment when Result.status changes to corrected. Also specify which value is authoritative during the sync window. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use as the concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, especially precedence and status interpretation. Source finding context: A Result changes to corrected between nightly synchronization runs while Report.result_status remains finalized or prelim. Source finding context: Downstream EMR/LIS consumers can simultaneously satisfy the batch rule and violate the maintained-same-information/amended obligation, making report status authority ambiguous for clinicians and integrations. Source finding context: The model combines immediate status-equivalence language with an explicitly delayed synchronization mechanism without defining the consistency window. Source finding context: Report.result_status is described as carrying the same result-state information clinicians trust. Source finding context: The correction rule requires Report.result_status to become amended when Result.status becomes corrected. Source finding context: The same rule states synchronization occurs only once nightly, enabling a period where the required amended state is not yet present.
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, especially status precedence and operational risk. Source finding context: Use the ontology as the concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, with attention to operational risk. Source finding context: Use as the concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration, especially precedence and status interpretation.
  - failure condition: A Result changes to corrected between nightly synchronization runs while Report.result_status has not yet become amended. Source finding context: A LIS result changes to `corrected` after report release but before the nightly synchronization batch updates the report status. Source finding context: A Result changes to corrected between nightly synchronization runs while Report.result_status remains finalized or prelim.
  - impact: Downstream EMR/LIS consumers and clinicians can trust a stale report status while the result has already been corrected, making status authority ambiguous. Source finding context: The EMR-facing status that clinicians are told to trust can remain stale, so downstream integration can present corrected clinical information as finalized during the lag window. Source finding context: Downstream EMR/LIS consumers can simultaneously satisfy the batch rule and violate the maintained-same-information/amended obligation, making report status authority ambiguous for clinicians and integrations.
  - root hypothesis: The model duplicates result and report status authority while allowing delayed reconciliation, leaving the authoritative state undefined during the correction window.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:69-83`, `materialized-input.md:121-123`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69-83,121-123; .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-11`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:72`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:83`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:72, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:80, .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-001`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-012`, `rel-001`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-001`, `finding-001.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:75-83`, `finding-001.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:69-72`, `finding-001.cause-003`, `finding-001.cause-004`, `finding-012.cause-001`, `finding-012.cause-002`, `finding-012.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-002`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-004`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, logic
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This is material because the ontology is intended to serve as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration, especially status precedence and operational risk. If the document permits a corrected Result.status while the report status clinicians are told to trust remains stale, it weakens the authority role by allowing downstream systems to present corrected clinical information as still finalized or preliminary.
  - root cause: The starting point is duplicated status authority: Result.status carries LIS result state while Report.result_status carries clinician-facing report state, but the model treats them as the same status information and also allows delayed reconciliation. Because no rule defines which value is authoritative during the lag, the correction path has no single source of truth.
  - causal path: The causal path is direct: Report.result_status is described as trusted by clinicians, Result.status separately records the LIS state, and a correction requires Report.result_status to become amended. The same rule allows synchronization only once nightly, creating a window where Result.status is corrected while Report.result_status has not yet become amended. That makes status interpretation ambiguous even when each local rule is followed.
  - action: Fix this before release by either defining one authoritative correction status or by making the eventual-consistency contract explicit. If delayed processing remains, the model needs a bounded stale interval, freshness timestamp, source result linkage, and EMR-facing authority rules such as an amendment_pending state so consumers know what to trust during the correction window.

- issue-006 (high): Orderable Test and executable Assay are modeled as parallel catalog structures without a governed mapping or authority boundary, so the ontology cannot reliably decide how an EMR orderable item corresponds to LIS/analyzer execution units when catalog items are added, changed, versioned, renamed, or retired.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 3/3 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage, evolution, semantics
  - remaining disagreement: 0/3 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage, evolution, semantics
  - issue statement: Orderable Test and executable Assay are parallel catalog-like structures without clear boundary, mapping authority, or effective history. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Test and Assay catalog authority Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-55, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121-123 Source finding context: Parallel Test and Assay catalogs are present without a single authority, mapping, or effective-history concept. Source finding context: Orderable and executable catalog concepts are both central to EMR/LIS integration. Without a canonical mapping and authority rule, the model covers the two catalog areas as parallel lists but not the cross-system reconciliation concepts needed when the same lab item is ordered, performed, renamed, versioned, or retired. Source finding context: Add a governed Test-Assay mapping concept with cardinality, effective_from/effective_to or version, authoritative source for shared attributes, lifecycle for catalog activation/retirement, and migration rules for items registered in both catalogs. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration where orderable tests must map to LIS/analyzer execution units. Source finding context: When a new or changed lab item exists in both Test and Assay with differing specimen kind, department, or name, the ontology lacks a canonical mapping and source of truth to decide integration behavior. Source finding context: The intended authority document leaves catalog reconciliation to future cleanup, allowing duplicate registration and divergent interpretations across EMR, LIS, analyzer, and dashboard consumers. Source finding context: The ontology intentionally maintains separate Test and Assay registrations without modeling the authority and history that make the parallel catalogs governable. Source finding context: Test and Assay both carry catalog-like attributes including code/name/display name, department, and specimen type/kind. Source finding context: The notes state new items are currently registered in both and catalog consolidation is deferred. Source finding context: No mapping, effective dating, lifecycle, or source-of-truth concept is defined for reconciling the parallel catalogs. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Test and Assay catalog model Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Test` has `test_code`, `department`, and enum `requires_specimen_type`; `Assay` has separate `assay_code`, `department`, and free-string `specimen_kind`; notes state "현재 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다." Source finding context: 신규 검사 항목 추가 시 `Test`와 `Assay`를 모두 수정해야 하므로 카탈로그 확장이 이중 등록에 의존한다. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서라면 새 검사 항목, 장비별 수행 단위, 패널/반사검사 같은 카탈로그 변화가 누락 없이 확장되어야 한다. 현재 모델은 `Test`와 `Assay`의 연속성을 관계로 보존하지 않고 병렬 속성(`department`, 검체 종류)을 중복 보유하므로 새 항목 추가 또는 카탈로그 정비 때 기존 구조와 데이터를 함께 고쳐야 하며, 두 목록이 어긋나도 어떤 쪽이 권위인지 판단할 수 없다. Source finding context: `Test`를 주문 카탈로그 권위로 둘지, 별도 `CatalogItem`/`Procedure` 같은 상위 개념을 둘지 정한 뒤 `Assay`가 그 권위를 참조하도록 관계를 추가한다. 검체 종류와 부서 같은 공유 속성은 한 권위 위치에서 파생되게 하고, 장비/방법/버전별 확장은 `Assay` 변형으로 추가되도록 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: EMR/LIS 연동 설계의 개념 권위 문서로서 검사 카탈로그와 수행 단위의 변경을 안정적으로 수용하는 목적. Source finding context: 신규 검사, 장비 수행 항목, 검체별 변형을 추가할 때 `Test`와 `Assay` 양쪽을 수동 등록하거나 기존 속성/관계를 재정비해야 하는 경우. Source finding context: 통합 설계에서 주문 코드와 수행 코드의 매핑 권위가 사라져 카탈로그 확장 때 데이터 불일치, 누락 등록, 기존 주문/결과 연결 단절 위험이 커진다. Source finding context: 검사 카탈로그 권위와 수행 단위 권위가 분리되어 있으나 둘을 잇는 명시 관계나 버전/매핑 개념이 없다. Source finding context: `Test`와 `Assay`가 각각 코드, 표시명/이름, 부서, 검체 관련 속성을 별도로 가진다. Source finding context: 문서의 현재 운영 규칙은 신규 항목을 두 곳 모두에 등록하게 한다. Source finding context: 따라서 새 검사 항목이나 수행 변형 추가는 단일 확장 지점이 아니라 기존 두 구조의 동시 수정과 정합성 유지에 의존한다. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:Test and Assay Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-55,121-122 Source finding context: Test와 Assay가 모두 검사 항목/카탈로그 단위로 정의되어 orderable catalog 개념과 executable assay 개념의 경계가 의미적으로 흐리다. Source finding context: 주문 가능한 검사와 분석기 수행 assay는 구분될 수 있지만, 현재 두 정의 모두 '검사 항목'과 '카탈로그' 의미를 공유하고 둘 사이의 의미 대응 규칙이 없습니다. 이 상태에서는 같은 검사를 두 개념에 중복 등록하는 것인지, 서로 다른 granularity의 개념인지 판단하기 어렵습니다. Source finding context: Test를 주문 가능 항목, Assay를 수행/분석 방법 또는 장비 수행 단위로 재정의하고 Test-Assay 매핑 관계와 cardinality를 명시하십시오. 두 개념이 실제로 동의어라면 하나의 canonical catalog concept으로 통합하고 alias만 남기십시오. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 검사 카탈로그와 수행 단위의 개념 권위를 제공해 EMR 주문과 LIS 수행을 연결하는 목적. Source finding context: 신규 항목이 Test와 Assay 양쪽에 등록되지만 두 이름의 의미 차이와 매핑이 정의되지 않을 때. Source finding context: EMR 주문 코드와 LIS/분석기 수행 코드가 같은 항목인지 다른 단계의 항목인지 불명확해 카탈로그 동기화와 결과 귀속에서 의미 드리프트가 생길 수 있습니다. Source finding context: 주문 가능한 검사 항목과 분석기 수행 항목의 개념 경계 및 매핑 권위가 정의되지 않았다. Source finding context: Test는 주문 가능한 카탈로그 단위인 단일 검사 항목으로 정의된다. Source finding context: Assay도 분석기에서 수행되는 검사 항목이자 검사 카탈로그의 수행 단위로 정의된다. Source finding context: 현재 신규 항목은 Test와 Assay 두 곳 모두에 등록한다고 notes가 말한다.
  - affected purpose: Use as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration where orderable tests must map to LIS/analyzer execution units. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 연동 설계의 개념 권위 문서로서 검사 카탈로그와 수행 단위의 변경을 안정적으로 수용하는 목적. Source finding context: 검사 카탈로그와 수행 단위의 개념 권위를 제공해 EMR 주문과 LIS 수행을 연결하는 목적.
  - failure condition: A new or changed lab item must be registered in both Test and Assay, or the two carry differing specimen kind, department, name, or code semantics. Source finding context: When a new or changed lab item exists in both Test and Assay with differing specimen kind, department, or name, the ontology lacks a canonical mapping and source of truth to decide integration behavior. Source finding context: 신규 검사, 장비 수행 항목, 검체별 변형을 추가할 때 `Test`와 `Assay` 양쪽을 수동 등록하거나 기존 속성/관계를 재정비해야 하는 경우. Source finding context: 신규 항목이 Test와 Assay 양쪽에 등록되지만 두 이름의 의미 차이와 매핑이 정의되지 않을 때.
  - impact: Catalog reconciliation is deferred to manual dual maintenance, allowing EMR, LIS, analyzer, and dashboard consumers to diverge. Source finding context: The intended authority document leaves catalog reconciliation to future cleanup, allowing duplicate registration and divergent interpretations across EMR, LIS, analyzer, and dashboard consumers. Source finding context: 통합 설계에서 주문 코드와 수행 코드의 매핑 권위가 사라져 카탈로그 확장 때 데이터 불일치, 누락 등록, 기존 주문/결과 연결 단절 위험이 커진다. Source finding context: EMR 주문 코드와 LIS/분석기 수행 코드가 같은 항목인지 다른 단계의 항목인지 불명확해 카탈로그 동기화와 결과 귀속에서 의미 드리프트가 생길 수 있습니다.
  - root hypothesis: Catalog extension relies on dual Test and Assay registration because the ontology lacks a governed Test-Assay mapping, source of truth, and effective-history concept.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:1-3`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-55`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121-123`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-55, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121-123`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Test` entity attributes`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Assay` entity attributes`, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes: "Test와 Assay는 카탈로그 정비 후 통합 여부를 재검토한다. 현재 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다."`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Test` has `test_code`, `department`, and enum `requires_specimen_type`; `Assay` has separate `assay_code`, `department`, and free-string `specimen_kind`; notes state "현재 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다."`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121-122`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-55,121-122`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-006`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-008`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-016`, `rel-009`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-009`, `rel-010`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-010`, `finding-006.cause-001`, `finding-006.cause-002`, `finding-006.cause-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:103-114`, `finding-008.cause-001`, `finding-008.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes: "현재 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다."`, `finding-008.cause-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes on dual registration`, `finding-016.cause-001`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41-47`, `finding-016.cause-002`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:49-55`, `finding-016.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.axiology`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-006`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-008`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage, evolution, semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because the ontology is meant to serve as concept authority for EMR/LIS integration. If Test and Assay both hold catalog-like attributes but no source of truth, cardinality, lifecycle, or effective history governs their relationship, EMR, LIS, analyzer, and dashboard consumers can interpret the same lab item differently and drift during catalog maintenance.
  - root cause: The starting point is the missing governed Test-Assay mapping and shared-attribute authority. Because the model first separates Test and Assay into distinct catalog-like structures, then requires new items to be registered in both, every downstream consistency problem follows from the absence of a canonical rule saying whether they are the same catalog item, different granularities, or linked orderable and executable units.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported across coverage, evolution, and semantics findings: Test and Assay both carry overlapping code/name, department, and specimen-related attributes; operational notes say new items are registered in both; and no mapping, effective dating, lifecycle, cardinality, or source-of-truth concept reconciles those parallel registrations. That makes additions or changes depend on manual dual maintenance and leaves order code versus execution code semantics unresolved.
  - action: Define the canonical catalog authority first, then add the Test-Assay mapping around it. The fix should specify orderable-versus-executable boundaries, mapping cardinality, authoritative ownership for shared attributes such as department and specimen kind, effective_from/effective_to or version history, and activation/retirement or migration rules for items currently present in both catalogs. This must be closed in the target model because later catalog, result, integration, and dashboard behavior depends on that authority.

- issue-010 (high): Result.status and Report.result_status are a material status-authority defect: they claim to carry the same status information, but use different vocabularies and meanings without a governed mapping, versioning, authority rule, or transition model.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, evolution, logic, semantics, structure

  - issue statement: Result.status and Report.result_status use different vocabularies while claiming to carry the same status information. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:Result.status and Report.result_status Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:64-78,116-118 Source finding context: Result.status와 Report.result_status가 '동일 정보'라고 정의되지만 서로 다른 상태 어휘와 권위 의미를 사용한다. Source finding context: 동일 의미의 상태라면 같은 개념 어휘이거나 명시적 의미 매핑이 있어야 합니다. 현재는 preliminary/prelim, final/finalized, corrected/amended가 암묵적으로 대응되는 듯하지만 이름과 상태 행위가 다릅니다. 특히 corrected는 결과 값의 정정 상태이고 amended는 보고서 개정 상태라서 같은 정보라고 보기 어렵습니다. EMR/LIS 통합 권위 문서에서 이 차이가 닫히지 않으면 어느 상태가 임상 표시와 주문 완료 판단의 의미 기준인지 흔들립니다. Source finding context: 결과 상태와 보고서 상태를 하나의 공통 상태 개념으로 통합하거나, ResultStatus와 ReportStatus를 분리해 각 값의 의미와 전이 매핑을 명시하십시오. '동일 정보'와 '권위 값' 중 어느 개념이 원천 권위인지도 하나로 정하십시오. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서로서 결과 상태와 보고 상태의 의미 기준을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: LIS Result.status와 EMR/임상 Report.result_status가 서로 다른 enum 이름과 의미를 사용하면서도 동일 정보 및 권위 값으로 선언될 때. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 preliminary/prelim, final/finalized, corrected/amended를 임의 매핑하거나 별도 권위 값으로 해석할 수 있어 임상의가 보는 최종/정정 상태와 주문 완료 판단의 의미가 달라질 수 있습니다. Source finding context: Result 상태 개념과 Report 상태 개념의 동일성 여부 및 권위 원천이 정의에서 분리되지 않았다. Source finding context: Result.status는 LIS가 기록하며 Report.result_status에도 동일 정보가 유지된다고 정의된다. Source finding context: Report.result_status는 별도 enum [prelim, finalized, amended]를 가지며 임상의가 신뢰하는 권위 값으로 정의된다. Source finding context: 상태 규칙은 Result.status corrected를 Report.result_status amended로 바꾼다고 하여 두 어휘 사이의 암묵 매핑에 의존한다. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / result and report status lifecycle Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Result.status` enum `[preliminary, final, corrected]`; `Report.result_status` enum `[prelim, finalized, amended]`; state rule says report becomes finalized at `released_at`, and corrected results require amended status via nightly batch. Source finding context: 결과 상태와 보고 상태가 별도 enum으로 중복되어 상태 체계 변경 시 기존 규칙의 연속성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 임상검사 보고 상태는 외부 시스템 통합과 규제/운영 변경에 따라 확장될 가능성이 높다. 현재 구조는 상태 권위가 결과와 보고서에 분산되어 있고 야간 배치 지연을 상태 모델 안에서 버전/전이/동기화 이벤트로 표현하지 않기 때문에, 새 상태 추가 시 기존 결과와 보고서의 의미 매핑이 끊길 수 있다. Source finding context: 상태 권위를 분리해 `ResultStatus`와 `ReportStatus`의 명시 매핑 테이블 또는 상태 전이 모델을 둔다. 상태 값에는 코드, 표시명, 버전, 유효기간, 파생/동기화 규칙을 포함하고, 배치 동기화는 이벤트나 SLA 속성으로 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-4d4a75e9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: 결과와 보고서 상태를 EMR/LIS 간에 장기적으로 일관되게 해석하고 확장하는 목적. Source finding context: 새 결과 상태나 보고 상태, 외부 시스템 상태 코드, 동기화 방식 변경이 추가되는 경우. Source finding context: 두 enum과 문장형 규칙을 동시에 수정해야 하며 기존 데이터의 상태 의미가 새 상태 체계로 어떻게 이어지는지 명확하지 않아 통합 신뢰가 약해진다. Source finding context: 상태 권위와 상태 매핑이 명시 개념으로 분리되지 않고 중복 enum 및 자연어 동기화 규칙에 묶여 있다. Source finding context: `Result.status`는 preliminary/final/corrected 값을 가진다. Source finding context: `Report.result_status`는 같은 축을 prelim/finalized/amended라는 별도 값으로 유지한다. Source finding context: 상태 동기화는 야간 1회 배치라는 자연어 규칙에 의존한다. Source finding context: 새 상태나 동기화 방식 추가 시 단일 상태 모델 확장이 아니라 두 enum과 배치 규칙의 동시 수정이 필요해진다.
  - affected purpose: Provide a stable concept authority for interpreting and extending result and report status across EMR/LIS systems. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서로서 결과 상태와 보고 상태의 의미 기준을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 결과와 보고서 상태를 EMR/LIS 간에 장기적으로 일관되게 해석하고 확장하는 목적.
  - failure condition: New result statuses, report statuses, external status codes, or synchronization modes are added or interpreted. Source finding context: LIS Result.status와 EMR/임상 Report.result_status가 서로 다른 enum 이름과 의미를 사용하면서도 동일 정보 및 권위 값으로 선언될 때. Source finding context: 새 결과 상태나 보고 상태, 외부 시스템 상태 코드, 동기화 방식 변경이 추가되는 경우.
  - impact: Implementers can map preliminary/prelim, final/finalized, and corrected/amended inconsistently, weakening clinical and workflow status trust. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 preliminary/prelim, final/finalized, corrected/amended를 임의 매핑하거나 별도 권위 값으로 해석할 수 있어 임상의가 보는 최종/정정 상태와 주문 완료 판단의 의미가 달라질 수 있습니다. Source finding context: 두 enum과 문장형 규칙을 동시에 수정해야 하며 기존 데이터의 상태 의미가 새 상태 체계로 어떻게 이어지는지 명확하지 않아 통합 신뢰가 약해진다.
  - root hypothesis: Status lifecycle extensibility is weakened because result and report states are duplicated as separate enums without a governed mapping, version, or transition model.

  - source lenses: semantics, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the ontology's purpose as a stable EMR/LIS concept authority because implementers cannot reliably know whether preliminary/prelim, final/finalized, and corrected/amended are identical states, mapped states, or separate authorities. That ambiguity can change what clinicians see as final or corrected and what workflows treat as order completion, especially when new statuses or synchronization modes are added.
  - root cause: The starting point is duplicated status authority: Result.status and Report.result_status represent the same lifecycle axis as separate enums, while the model does not define whether one is authoritative, whether they are distinct concepts, or how their values map and evolve. Without that authority and mapping layer, every downstream status rule inherits the ambiguity.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by the observed model shape: Result.status carries preliminary/final/corrected, Report.result_status carries prelim/finalized/amended, and the corrected-to-amended relationship is expressed through a natural-language nightly-batch rule. That means current interpretation depends on implicit mappings, and future changes require coordinated edits to two enums plus synchronization prose rather than extending a single governed status model or explicit transition table.
  - action: Fix this before release by either unifying result and report status under one common status concept, or explicitly separating ResultStatus and ReportStatus with governed mappings, authority ownership, versions, valid periods, transition rules, and synchronization events or SLA semantics. The key ordering dependency is to decide the source of status authority first, then encode mappings and transitions from that authority so clinical display, correction handling, and order-completion logic all read the same lifecycle semantics.

- issue-002 (medium): Critical-value notification is materially under-modeled: the ontology uses a bare `notified` boolean where EMR/LIS integration needs an auditable notification event with recipient, notifier, time, channel, acknowledgement, and source authority.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, structure
